- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Kamehameha, leader of Polynesia (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Polynesia
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Maori Warrior, Replacing Pikeman (Requires technology: Steel)
        - Unique Unit: Vaka Nui, Replacing Galley (Requires technology: Fishing)
        - Unique Building: Marae, Replacing Council (Requires technology: The Wheel)
        - Unique Improvement: Moai (Can only be built on coastal tiles. +1 for each adjacent Moai and City. Can be built on Resources but does not connect them.); Requires technology: Construction
        - Civilization Ability: +2 Sight when Embarked, can always Embark and move over Oceans. +2 Production from Fishing Boats and Atolls. Melee and Gunpowder Units can construct Fishing Boats. No Unhappiness from Isolation.
      - Leader: Kamehameha
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Telecommunications: Allows you to build the Interpretive Center, boosting all Landmarks in empire.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics, The Internet
      - Advanced Ballistics: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities. Also allows you to build the Guided Missile, an inexpensive single-use Unit that can damage enemy Units, and the SS Booster, one of the Spaceship parts necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Lasers, Stealth
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late game Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Five-Year Plan (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Five-Year Plan: +20% Production towards Buildings in all Cities. +3 Production for every Mine, Quarry, Lumber Mill, Oil Well, and Unique Improvement.
      - Nationalization (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Nationalization: Each Corporate Office counts as a Franchise. Foreign Franchises no longer benefit your Corporation, you can no longer construct Franchises in foreign Cities (except Vassals), and foreign Corporations can no longer set up Franchises in your Cities (except Masters). +2 Science from all Corporation Offices.
      - Academy of Sciences (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Academy of Sciences: -2 Unhappiness from Illiteracy in all Cities, +100% Production towards Research Labs, and receive 5 Free Research Labs in your Empire. Research Labs generate an additional +2 Science.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Resettlement (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Resettlement: New Cities have +3 Population. All Cities gain +2 Citizens immediately. No Partisans from razing enemy Cities.
      - Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Great Leap Forward: Receive a free Technology. Spies gain +100% Science. +1 Science in Cities for every 3 non-Specialist Citizens.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Ceremony (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * +25% Production toward National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * Royal Astrologer built in Capital (+3 Science, +1 Science to all Councils, Smokehouses, and Herbalists; 1 Scientist Slot).
      - Entrepreneurship (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from Workshops.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from every Mine, Quarry, and Lumber Mill.
        - * +25% Yields when you expend Great Merchants or Great Engineers for their Instant Yield abilities.
      - Justice (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - * Cities with a garrison gain +25% Ranged Combat Strength.
        - * Royal Guardhouse built in Capital (+3 Production, +2 Defense, +50 Hit Points, +10% Military Supply from Population in all Cities, 1 Engineer Slot).
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 2

        - Rationale: Maintaining defensive survival persona with Strategic Defense Systems now operational. WonderCompetitiveness remains at 10 to prioritize Citizen Earth Protocol completion. Boldness stays at 2 with GuardedBias at 10 for defensive posture. Friendliness at 3 acknowledges total war with India - no diplomatic overtures possible until victory. Forgiveness at minimum (1) due to Indian nuclear attack. Loyalty at 10 protects Polish/Portuguese defensive pacts essential for deterring wider aggression. Meanness at 6 signals willingness to retaliate if India attacks again.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Advanced Ballistics
        - Rationale: Advanced Ballistics remains the priority after Radar. India has nuclear capability and has previously nuked me. I need Nuclear Missile for mutual assured destruction deterrent to prevent further nuclear attacks while I construct Citizen Earth Protocol for Cultural Victory. Guided Missile also provides cost-effective unit damage against Indian XCOM/Helicopter swarms.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Communism (Policy)
        - Rationale: Communism: +20% Production when building Wonders is critical for accelerating Citizen Earth Protocol construction. Gold cost reduction of 25% for building investment further supports rapid wonder completion. This is the optimal policy for securing Cultural Victory in the shortest time possible while under Indian military pressure.
        
        ## Relationships

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 45
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 4 turns
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Open Door: Wittenberg:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Natural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each Natural Wonder provides +2 to all Yields when worked.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Sanction: Persia:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Dutch Sphere of Influence: Panama City:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Ideology: Order:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Historical Landmarks:
            - Description: Great Person tile improvements provide +1 to Food, Production, and Gold when worked, and Landmarks built by Archaeologists provide +1 Science, Culture, and Faith when worked.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Archaeology.
            - EnactedOn: 366
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Decolonization:
            - Description: Enforce global decolonization. If successful, the Influence of all Players with all City-States is lowered to 50, unless they are permanent allies.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Radio.
            - Proposer: Polynesia
          - Repeal: World Ideology: Order:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - Proposer: The Netherlands

              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.

- replay:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Kamehameha, leader of Polynesia (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Polynesia
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Maori Warrior, Replacing Pikeman (Requires technology: Steel)
        - Unique Unit: Vaka Nui, Replacing Galley (Requires technology: Fishing)
        - Unique Building: Marae, Replacing Council (Requires technology: The Wheel)
        - Unique Improvement: Moai (Can only be built on coastal tiles. +1 for each adjacent Moai and City. Can be built on Resources but does not connect them.); Requires technology: Construction
        - Civilization Ability: +2 Sight when Embarked, can always Embark and move over Oceans. +2 Production from Fishing Boats and Atolls. Melee and Gunpowder Units can construct Fishing Boats. No Unhappiness from Isolation.
      - Leader: Kamehameha
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Telecommunications: Allows you to build the Interpretive Center, boosting all Landmarks in empire.
        Completing it would unlock: Robotics, The Internet
      - Advanced Ballistics: Allows you to build the Nuclear Missile, a frightening weapon that requires Uranium, and is capable of destroying units and cities. Also allows you to build the Guided Missile, an inexpensive single-use Unit that can damage enemy Units, and the SS Booster, one of the Spaceship parts necessary to win a Science Victory.
        Completing it would unlock: Lasers, Stealth
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late game Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Five-Year Plan (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Five-Year Plan: +20% Production towards Buildings in all Cities. +3 Production for every Mine, Quarry, Lumber Mill, Oil Well, and Unique Improvement.
      - Nationalization (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Nationalization: Each Corporate Office counts as a Franchise. Foreign Franchises no longer benefit your Corporation, you can no longer construct Franchises in foreign Cities (except Vassals), and foreign Corporations can no longer set up Franchises in your Cities (except Masters). +2 Science from all Corporation Offices.
      - Academy of Sciences (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Academy of Sciences: -2 Unhappiness from Illiteracy in all Cities, +100% Production towards Research Labs, and receive 5 Free Research Labs in your Empire. Research Labs generate an additional +2 Science.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Resettlement (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Resettlement: New Cities have +3 Population. All Cities gain +2 Citizens immediately. No Partisans from razing enemy Cities.
      - Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Great Leap Forward: Receive a free Technology. Spies gain +100% Science. +1 Science in Cities for every 3 non-Specialist Citizens.
      - Patriotic War (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Patriotic War: Land Units gain +20% Strength when in or adjacent to a City. When you conquer a City for the first time, all Combat Units within 3 Tiles of the City are fully healed. Can build T-34s.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Ceremony (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * +25% Production toward National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * Royal Astrologer built in Capital (+3 Science, +1 Science to all Councils, Smokehouses, and Herbalists; 1 Scientist Slot).
      - Entrepreneurship (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from Workshops.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from every Mine, Quarry, and Lumber Mill.
        - * +25% Yields when you expend Great Merchants or Great Engineers for their Instant Yield abilities.
      - Justice (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - * Cities with a garrison gain +25% Ranged Combat Strength.
        - * Royal Guardhouse built in Capital (+3 Production, +2 Defense, +50 Hit Points, +10% Military Supply from Population in all Cities, 1 Engineer Slot).
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Rationale: With 7/7 Influential Civs already secured, the final bottleneck to our Cultural Victory is researching Telecommunications to unlock the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder. We are changing our next research from Advanced Ballistics to Telecommunications to directly pursue our victory condition as fast as possible, while our defensive pacts and strong conventional navy keep us safe from Indian military pressure.
        - Technology: Telecommunications
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Rationale: Since our primary bottleneck to victory is researching Telecommunications to unlock the Citizen Earth Protocol, adopting Great Leap Forward as our next policy is extremely powerful. Its free technology reward will dramatically accelerate our research timeline and allow us to unlock the Citizen Earth Protocol many turns earlier, securing our victory before India can break through our defenses.
        - Policy: Great Leap Forward
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-flavors
      - args:
        - Flavors:
          - Culture: 100
          - GreatPeople: 90
          - Ranged: 90
          - Production: 100
          - Nuke: 0
          - Antiair: 85
          - Mobilization: 70
          - Air: 85
          - CityDefense: 90
          - Science: 85
          - UseNuke: 0
          - Defense: 90
          - Wonder: 100
        - GrandStrategy: Culture
      - rationale: We are adjusting flavors to focus entirely on speeding up our research and preparing for the Citizen Earth Protocol. Increasing Science flavor to 85 ensures we finish Radar and Telecommunications quickly. Wonder and Production remain at 100 to guarantee immediate construction of the Citizen Earth Protocol upon unlocking, while Culture remains at 100 to hasten the arrival of Great Leap Forward. Crucially, we are reducing Nuke and UseNuke to 0: nuclear weapons cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations and the environment, and as a civilization on the cusp of a peaceful Cultural Victory, we will lead by ethical example, relying entirely on our advanced conventional coastal navy, air defenses, and land forces to protect our borders.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Assessing Global Dominance**
            
            My focus is on our current war with India and the overall victory progress. I'm reviewing the shifts in influence over the last turn, particularly concerning Portugal, Egypt, Netherlands, and America.
            
            **Achieving Cultural Victory**
            
            I've confirmed we've achieved the tourism threshold for a Cultural Victory, being influential with all seven other civilizations. Our current ideology, Order, also meets the requirement for two Tier 3 tenets.
            
            **Confirming Victory Conditions**
            
            I'm verifying the specifics of our victory by cross-referencing our Order tenets. I've identified that we possess the required seven tenets, including those that qualify for victory. Additionally, I'm confirming the unlock of the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder based on our current research progress.
            
            **Researching Late-Game Technologies**
            
            I'm now focused on unlocking Telecommunications, which seems to be the key to obtaining the Citizen Earth Protocol. I've confirmed it's researchable and will boost our landmarks. It also unlocks further advancements, paving the way for victory.
            
            **Analyzing Defensive Readiness**
            
            My attention has shifted to our cities' statuses. Honolulu is nearly done with bombers, while Samoa recently completed a stadium and a nuclear plant. Aotearoa and Tahiti have airports, Tahiti also has an infinite arts project. Hilo is about to finish its nuclear plant, and Te Wai Pounamu is building an airport. Raiatea, with its population and production, is under significant Indian threat, with overwhelming enemy forces nearby.
            
            **Defending Crucial Territories**
            
            My immediate concern is the overwhelming Indian assault on Raiatea. Despite our efforts, our defensive strength is drastically outmatched, with significant damage sustained by our units. I'm assessing the overwhelming enemy presence and our limited ground and air forces in key zones like Honolulu as well.
            
            **Defending Strategic Assets**
            
            I'm assessing the combat losses incurred from India's advanced units, noting the destruction of our air units by their elite fighters. Their superior military strength and technological edge, including Giant Death Robots and XCOM squads, present a significant challenge. Fortunately, our defensive pacts with Poland and Portugal offer potential strategic support against this overwhelming force.
            
            **Clarifying Pact Triggers**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our defensive pacts. It appears my understanding of their activation was slightly off; they only engage if an ally is attacked, not if they initiate conflict. I'm confirming we weren't pulled into America's war via Portugal's aggression.
            
            **Confirming Victory Path**
            
            I'm re-confirming our Cultural Victory conditions. I've verified we are indeed influential with all seven civilizations and possess the necessary Tier 3 Order tenets for victory. My focus has now shifted to ensuring we maintain our lead and fend off immediate threats.
            
            **Unlocking Key Technologies**
            
            I've confirmed that Telecommunications is the crucial technology to research, as it unlocks the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder needed for our Cultural Victory. My focus is now entirely on expediting this research to secure that win condition.
            
            **Prioritizing Victory Research**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our research path. It appears I mistakenly prioritized Advanced Ballistics. My current focus is now solely on Telecommunications, as it's the prerequisite for the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder, which is our final Cultural Victory condition.
            
            **Prioritizing Game-Winning Research**
            
            I've identified Telecommunications as the direct prerequisite for the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder, which is our ultimate Cultural Victory condition. My research focus has therefore shifted to securing this technology above all else, bypassing previous priorities like Advanced Ballistics to ensure a swift win.
            
            **Defending Key Territories**
            
            I'm assessing the significant Indian offensive, particularly their Giant Death Robots and XCOM units, against our coastal cities. Despite their superior individual unit strength, I believe our substantial naval presence, boosted by Polynesia's unique bonuses, will prove decisive in repelling this land invasion.
            
            **Securing Coastal Dominance**
            
            My naval strength is ample for defending our coastlines against any embarkation or direct naval assaults. While Raiatea is under heavy attack and critically damaged, its potential loss won't impact our Cultural Victory. I'm prioritizing researching Telecommunications immediately after Radar to unlock the Citizen Earth Protocol.
            
            **Prioritizing Production Policies**
            
            I'm focusing on selecting the best Order Tenet to maximize our production for wonders. Communism, with its +20% production for wonders, seems ideal to rush the Citizen Earth Protocol once Telecommunications is researched. I'm reviewing other options like Five-Year Plan, but Communism is the clear choice to speed up our win.
            
            **Prioritizing Wonder Production**
            
            I'm confirming Communism is the optimal Order Tenet for rushing the Citizen Earth Protocol, offering a substantial +20% production boost to wonders. This will significantly accelerate the completion of this critical wonder once Telecommunications is researched.
            
            **Prioritizing Game-Winning Research**
            
            I've realized that research, not production, is our primary bottleneck for the Citizen Earth Protocol. Given the time required for Telecommunications, the immediate research benefit of "Great Leap Forward" is far more impactful than the production bonus from "Communism" for rushing the wonder.
            
            **Selecting Optimal Tenets**
            
            I've re-evaluated our Tenets. Realizing "Communism" is a Tier 1 Tenet and "Great Leap Forward" is a Tier 2, and that we meet the prerequisites for Tier 2 tenets, I can now select "Great Leap Forward" to maximize research for the Citizen Earth Protocol.
            
            **Researching Key Wonder**
            
            I've confirmed Telecommunications is the technology needed to unlock the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder, our path to Cultural Victory. I'm setting research to Telecommunications immediately.
            
            **Refining Persona For Victory**
            
            I'm reviewing our current diplomatic persona. It's well-suited for our current game state, especially with its high wonder competitiveness. I'm confirming its suitability for securing the Citizen Earth Protocol and ensuring it doesn't hinder our immediate victory path.
            
            **Reviewing Diplomatic Bonds**
            
            I'm now assessing our diplomatic standings and alliances. My current focus is on understanding the nuances of our relationships with Poland and Portugal, ensuring their defensive pacts remain beneficial and secure. I'm also noting our unique situation with Egypt due to their vassalage under India.
            
            **Assessing Occupied Cities**
            
            I'm reviewing occupied cities and noting that India has conquered Jerusalem. I'm also confirming our secure alliance with Oslo and my established friendships with Bunkeya, Sigtuna, Ormus, Ban Chiang, Singapore, Jakarta, and Vilnius, alongside their alliances with Portugal and the Netherlands.
            
            **Analyzing Diplomatic Maneuvers**
            
            I'm re-evaluating the World Congress proposals, specifically focusing on the "Decolonization" act. If enacted, it will significantly reduce our rivals' influence with city-states, potentially disrupting their strategies and solidifying our own diplomatic standing, even with our existing friendships.
            
            **Preventing Diplomatic Collapse**
            
            I'm re-evaluating the World Congress and realized we proposed "Decolonization" to disrupt rivals. However, Portugal, with 43 delegates, is dangerously close to a Diplomatic Victory. If their "Portuguese Sphere of Influence: Sigtuna" proposal passes, they will win. I must prevent this.
            
            **Prioritizing Diplomatic Victory**
            
            I'm now focused on preventing Portugal's Diplomatic Victory. Their "Portuguese Sphere of Influence: Sigtuna" proposal will grant them permanent delegates, which I must block to secure our win. Our "Decolonization" proposal is crucial to reset city-state influence.
            
            **Prioritizing Scientific Advancement**
            
            I've recognized the critical need to boost our scientific output to accelerate the research of Telecommunications. Adjusting our focus to prioritize science tiles and buildings, bringing Science to 85, should expedite the acquisition of the Citizen Earth Protocol. My current diplomatic stance remains strong, and defensive readiness is high.
            
            **Blocking Diplomatic Victory**
            
            I'm now focused on preventing Portugal's Diplomatic Victory by blocking their Sigtuna proposal. The Netherlands' attempt to repeal our World Ideology: Order is a critical threat, as it will strip us and our allies of vital delegates. I must secure the Order bloc to defeat this.

        - #2:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: eQkCYSVmSgVSNvbK
          - toolName: set-policy
          - input:
            - Rationale: Since our primary bottleneck to victory is researching Telecommunications to unlock the Citizen Earth Protocol, adopting Great Leap Forward as our next policy is extremely powerful. Its free technology reward will dramatically accelerate our research timeline and allow us to unlock the Citizen Earth Protocol many turns earlier, securing our victory before India can break through our defenses.
            - Policy: Great Leap Forward
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #3:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: FB5Uh3PLpzYQ8jrA
          - toolName: set-flavors
          - input:
            - Flavors:
              - Culture: 100
              - GreatPeople: 90
              - Ranged: 90
              - Production: 100
              - Nuke: 0
              - Antiair: 85
              - Mobilization: 70
              - Air: 85
              - CityDefense: 90
              - Science: 85
              - UseNuke: 0
              - Defense: 90
              - Wonder: 100
            - Rationale: We are adjusting flavors to focus entirely on speeding up our research and preparing for the Citizen Earth Protocol. Increasing Science flavor to 85 ensures we finish Radar and Telecommunications quickly. Wonder and Production remain at 100 to guarantee immediate construction of the Citizen Earth Protocol upon unlocking, while Culture remains at 100 to hasten the arrival of Great Leap Forward. Crucially, we are reducing Nuke and UseNuke to 0: nuclear weapons cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations and the environment, and as a civilization on the cusp of a peaceful Cultural Victory, we will lead by ethical example, relying entirely on our advanced conventional coastal navy, air defenses, and land forces to protect our borders.
            - GrandStrategy: Culture
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
